- **sddw-chat**: Fast-track interaction with a feature that already has artifacts. Skips the full questionnaire ceremony. Supports --auto flag to remove even scope-change confirmations. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-chat) · ★3)
- **sddw-code-analysis**: Analyse the existing codebase to extract patterns, interfaces, flows, and conventions. Use when the user says "sddw code analysis", "analyse the codebase", or wants to ground design decisions in existing code before running sddw design. This is an optional step between Requirements and Design. Produces .sddw/code-analysis.md (shared across features). Supports --auto flag for fully autonomous mode. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-code-analysis) · ★3)
- **sddw-design**: Generate the cross-cutting design.md for a feature — architecture, data models, interface contracts, and design decisions. Use when the user says "sddw design", "design the architecture", or wants to create a technical design document. This is Step 3 of the sddw workflow. Requires requirements.md to exist. Produces .sddw/&lt;feature&gt;/design/design.md. Supports --auto flag for fully autonomous mode. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-design) · ★3)
- **sddw-design-and-taskify**: Combined design and task decomposition in one flow. Use when the user says "sddw design and taskify", "design and break down tasks", or wants the one-shot path for smaller features. Produces both design.md and task files. Requires requirements.md. Supports --auto flag for fully autonomous mode. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-design-and-taskify) · ★3)
- **sddw-help**: Show sddw workflow overview, list all features with progress indicators, or show detailed status for a specific feature. Use when the user says "sddw help", "sddw list", "sddw status", "show features", or wants to see workflow documentation or feature progress. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-help) · ★3)
- **sddw-implement**: Implement a single task from the design spec following TDD, commit, and deviation handling protocols. Use when the user says "sddw implement", "implement task N", "execute task", or wants to code a specific task from the sddw pipeline. Requires design.md and task files to exist. Supports --task N flag and --auto flag. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-implement) · ★3)
- **sddw-requirements**: Generate a requirements specification for a feature using the sddw (Spec-Driven Development Workflow) pipeline. Produces .sddw/&lt;feature&gt;/requirements.md with Purpose, User Stories, Functional Requirements, Acceptance Criteria, and Constraints. Supports --auto flag for fully autonomous mode. ([christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini](https://github.com/christianmerkwirth/sddw-gemini/tree/HEAD/jetski-skills/sddw-requirements) · ★3)
- **sddw-self-improve**: Analyse a completed feature's execution across all workflow steps and propose concrete improvements to the sddw workflow itself. - **sddw-task-review**: Review a single completed implementation task before moving on — a focused code review against the task's done/acceptance criteria, design.md contracts, and codebase conventions. - **sddw-taskify**: Break a feature into hybrid task files from an existing design.md.
